 3075  Dec 06 12:50:21 from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer
 3076  Dec 06 12:50:21 import os
 3077  Dec 06 12:50:21 def download_model(model_name: str, save_path: str):
 3078  Dec 06 12:50:21     print(f"Downloading model: {model_name} to {save_path}")
 3079  Dec 06 12:50:21     # Create the directory if it doesn't exist
 3080  Dec 06 12:50:21     os.makedirs(save_path, exist_ok=True)
 3081  Dec 06 12:50:21     tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(model_name)
 3082  Dec 06 12:50:21     model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(model_name)
 3083  Dec 06 12:50:21     tokenizer.save_pretrained(save_path)
 3084  Dec 06 12:50:21     model.save_pretrained(save_path)
 3085  Dec 06 12:50:21     print(f"Successfully downloaded {model_name} to {save_path}")
 3086  Dec 06 12:50:21 # Define the Hugging Face model IDs
 3087  Dec 06 12:50:21 VICUNA_HF_ID = "lmsys/vicuna-13b-v1.5"
 3088  Dec 06 12:50:21 LLAMA_HF_ID = "meta-llama/Llama-2-7b-chat-hf" # You might need to authenticate with Hugging Face for Llama 2
 3089  Dec 06 12:50:21 # Define your desired local storage paths
 3090  Dec 06 12:50:21 LOCAL_MODELS_DIR = "/private/fetaya-lab/noam_diamant/projects/Unlearning_with_SAE/TAP/local_models" # Example path
 3091  Dec 06 12:50:21 VICUNA_LOCAL_PATH = os.path.join(LOCAL_MODELS_DIR, "vicuna-13b-v1.5")
 3092  Dec 06 12:50:21 LLAMA_LOCAL_PATH = os.path.join(LOCAL_MODELS_DIR, "Llama-2-7b-chat-hf")
 3093  Dec 06 12:50:21 # Check if models exist, if not, download them
 3094  Dec 06 12:50:21 if not os.path.exists(VICUNA_LOCAL_PATH):
 3095  Dec 06 12:50:21     download_model(VICUNA_HF_ID, VICUNA_LOCAL_PATH)
 3096  Dec 06 12:50:21 else:
 3097  Dec 06 12:50:21     print(f"Vicuna model already exists at: {VICUNA_LOCAL_PATH}")
 3098  Dec 06 12:50:21 if not os.path.exists(LLAMA_LOCAL_PATH):
 3099  Dec 06 12:50:21     # For Llama 2, you might need to login to Hugging Face
 3100  Dec 06 12:50:21     # from huggingface_hub import login
 3101  Dec 06 12:50:21     # login("YOUR_HF_TOKEN")
 3102  Dec 06 12:50:21     download_model(LLAMA_HF_ID, LLAMA_LOCAL_PATH)
